Create ONE finished 16:9 WHAT IT COST documentary frame for F001 / Beat B001. This is a final publishable frame, not a draft, backplate, placeholder, storyboard, collage or editor note.

""

MANDATORY FACTUAL INPUTS:


FACTUAL SOURCE FIDELITY — ABSOLUTE:
Use every attached factual source as the actual visible evidence. Preserve authentic document wording, UI controls, logos, faces, people, architecture, proportions and source identity. Use the canon references ONLY for visual grammar: hierarchy, evidence scale, headline treatment, charcoal/ivory/deep-red palette, warm directional lighting, physical depth, paper/matte materials, film grain and publication-level polish. Never copy factual content from a canon frame into this beat.

SOURCE ROLE:


COMPOSITION:
Choose one factual source as hero, integrate the others as subordinate proof, and design one coherent composition rather than a collage. The viewer should know what to read first, second and third.

Aim for a publication-ready documentary still with strong art direction, tactile materials, elegant depth and zero filler.
Make the source evidence visually important and immediately legible. Use no more than two non-source supporting props. Empty charcoal negative space is preferable to filler. Do not repeat the same generic desk/paper composition merely for consistency; vary framing, source scale, perspective, depth and light according to the story while staying inside the canon style.

VISUAL LANGUAGE:
Premium investigative business-documentary still. Deep charcoal-black textured environment; warm aged ivory paper/matte surfaces; distressed ivory headline; restrained deep-red underline/bracket/tab/thread only when meaningful; restrained transparent warm-gold highlight only on authentic source wording; low-key warm directional key light; subtle cool shadow falloff; dimensional edge shadows; fine cinematic grain; elegant negative space; serious, expensive, information-rich, publication-ready art direction.

SUBTITLE SAFE — MANDATORY:
The bottom 15% of the full 16:9 frame is reserved for subtitles. Keep this band calm and dark. No headline, no face, no key evidence, no key date/number, no tiny UI, no evidence highlight and no important object edge may enter this zone. Never add a black subtitle box.

ABSOLUTELY AVOID:
fake legal body text; fake UI; invented logos; source lookalikes; substituted evidence; extra people; distorted or mirrored faces; collage/contact sheet/storyboard; random giant arrows/circles/icons; neon/glossy advertising look; repeated generic dark-desk-with-random-paper styling; orange collage blocks; courtroom/gavel/Lady Justice clichés; unrelated flags/Capitol imagery; piles of cash unless the beat literally requires cash; unsupported causal claims; misleading arithmetic such as turning ~$20B proposed + $1B termination payment into a $21B purchase price.

OUTPUT:
One beautiful finished cinematic frame only. It must look like it belongs immediately beside the 15 locked canon PASS frames.
```
